DIL treats LLMs, the Web, and the User as source 'databases', each with their own query interface, elevating them to first-class data sources. DIL relies on data planners to transform user queries into executable query plans. These plans are declarative abstractions that unify relational operators with other operators spanning multiple modalities. DIL planners support decomposition of complex requests into subqueries, retrieval from diverse sources, and finally reasoning and integration to produce final results. We demonstrate DIL through two interactive scenarios in which user queries dynamically trigger multi-source retrieval, cross-modal reasoning, and result synthesis, illustrating how compound AI systems can move beyond single database NL2SQL.

</details>


### [184] [How Do LLMs and VLMs Understand Viewpoint Rotation Without Vision? An Interpretability Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15294)
*Zhen Yang,Ping Jian,Zhongbin Guo,Zuming Zhang,Chengzhi Li,Yonghong Deng,Xinyue Zhang,Wenpeng Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了视角旋转理解（VRU）任务，发现当前LLM和VLM在纯文本输入下表现不佳，并通过探测和因果干预分析揭示了模型在视点与观测绑定上的缺陷，最后通过选择性微调关键注意力头提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多从视觉-空间角度研究空间智能，但在缺乏视觉信息时，仅凭语言智能是否足以赋予模型空间智能，以及模型如何处理纯文本输入的空间任务，尚待探索。本文聚焦于语言视角下的空间智能基本能力——视角旋转理解（VRU）。

Method: 构建VRU数据集，要求LLM和VLM根据多步视角旋转的文本描述推断最终视角并预测观测内容。采用层级探测分析和头级因果干预，揭示模型内部机制。基于因果干预识别的关键注意力头，进行选择性微调。

Result: LLM和VLM在VRU任务上表现均较差，而人类可轻松达到100%准确率，显示出显著能力差距。分析发现模型虽在隐藏状态中编码了视角信息，但难以将视角位置与相应观测绑定，导致最终层产生幻觉。选择性微调关键注意力头有效提升了VRU性能，且避免了通用能力的灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 当前模型在纯文本空间推理任务上存在明显不足，核心问题在于视点与观测的绑定能力缺失。通过机制分析与选择性微调可针对性提升空间智能，为未来语言模型的空间能力研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: Over the past year, spatial intelligence has drawn increasing attention. Many prior works study it from the perspective of visual-spatial intelligence, where models have access to visuospatial information from visual inputs. However, in the absence of visual information, whether linguistic intelligence alone is sufficient to endow models with spatial intelligence, and how models perform relevant tasks with text-only inputs still remain unexplored. Therefore, in this paper, we focus on a fundamental and critical capability in spatial intelligence from a linguistic perspective: viewpoint rotation understanding (VRU). Specifically, LLMs and VLMs are asked to infer their final viewpoint and predict the corresponding observation in an environment given textual description of viewpoint rotation and observation over multiple steps. We find that both LLMs and VLMs perform poorly on our proposed dataset while human can easily achieve 100% accuracy, indicating a substantial gap between current model capabilities and the requirements of spatial intelligence. To uncover the underlying mechanisms, we conduct a layer-wise probing analysis and head-wise causal intervention. Our findings reveal that although models encode viewpoint information in the hidden states, they appear to struggle to bind the viewpoint position with corresponding observation, resulting in a hallucination in final layers. Finally, we selectively fine-tune the key attention heads identified by causal intervention to improve VRU performance. Experimental results demonstrate that such selective fine-tuning achieves improved VRU performance while avoiding catastrophic forgetting of generic abilities. Our dataset and code will be released at https://github.com/Young-Zhen/VRU_Interpret .

</details>


### [185] [Diagnosing LLM Judge Reliability: Conformal Prediction Sets and Transitivity Violations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15302)
*Manan Gupta,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个双管齐下的诊断工具包，用于评估LLM-as-judge框架的逐实例可靠性，发现虽然总体违规率低，但33-67%的文档存在传递性不一致，且预测集宽度可作为可靠的逐实例难度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-judge框架被广泛用于自动NLG评估，但其逐实例可靠性（per-instance reliability）仍然知之甚少。现有的评估方法可能掩盖了输入级别的不一致性问题，因此需要开发能够揭示这种隐藏不一致性的诊断工具。

Method: 提出两种互补的诊断方法：(1) 传递性分析（transitivity analysis）：检测成对比较中的有向3循环，揭示被低聚合违规率掩盖的逐输入不一致性；(2) 分割共形预测集（split conformal prediction sets）：为1-5 Likert分数提供理论保证的≥(1-α)覆盖率，将集宽作为逐实例可靠性指标。在SummEval数据集上对四个评判者和四个标准进行实验验证。

Result: 传递性分析显示33-67%的文档存在至少一个有向3循环，尽管总体违规率仅为0.8-4.1%；预测集宽度与可靠性显著相关（r_s = +0.576, p < 10^-100）；预测集宽度在不同评判者间显示出一致的交叉协议（r̄ = 0.32-0.38），证明其捕获的是文档级难度而非评判者特定噪声；不同标准的可靠性差异显著：相关性最可靠（平均集大小≈3.0），连贯性中等（≈3.9），流畅性和一致性不可靠（≈4.9）。

Conclusion: 标准（criterion）的选择比评判者（judge）的选择更重要；传递性分析和共形预测集宽度作为诊断工具具有互补性和一致性；该方法为LLM-as-judge框架的可靠性评估提供了理论保证和实用工具；所有代码、提示和缓存结果已公开。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge frameworks are increasingly used for automatic NLG evaluation, yet their per-instance reliability remains poorly understood. We present a two-pronged diagnostic toolkit applied to SummEval: $\textbf{(1)}$ a transitivity analysis that reveals widespread per-input inconsistency masked by low aggregate violation rates ($\barρ = 0.8$-$4.1\%$), with $33$-$67\%$ of documents exhibiting at least one directed 3-cycle; and $\textbf{(2)}$ split conformal prediction sets over 1-5 Likert scores providing theoretically-guaranteed $\geq(1{-}α)$ coverage, with set width serving as a per-instance reliability indicator ($r_s = {+}0.576$, $N{=}1{,}918$, $p < 10^{-100}$, pooled across all judges). Critically, prediction set width shows consistent cross-judge agreement ($\bar{r} = 0.32$-$0.38$), demonstrating it captures document-level difficulty rather than judge-specific noise. Across four judges and four criteria, both diagnostics converge: criterion matters more than judge, with relevance judged most reliably (avg. set size $\approx 3.0$) and coherence moderately so (avg. set size $\approx 3.9$), while fluency and consistency remain unreliable (avg. set size $\approx 4.9$). We release all code, prompts, and cached results.

</details>


### [186] [Generalization in LLM Problem Solving: The Case of the Shortest Path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15306)
*Yao Tong,Jiayuan Ye,Anastasia Borovykh,Reza Shokri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过可控合成环境分离影响模型泛化的多因素，发现模型在空间迁移上表现良好但在长度扩展上失败，揭示了数据覆盖决定能力上限，强化学习提升稳定性但不扩展上限，推理时优化无法解决长度扩展失败的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的系统性泛化能力存在争议，但实证性能受训练数据、训练范式和推理策略等多因素共同影响，导致难以解释失败原因，因此需要一种能够清晰分离这些因素的研究环境。

Method: 构建基于最短路径规划的可控合成环境，实现因素分离并支持两种正交的泛化测试轴：向未见地图的空间迁移和向更长时序问题的长度扩展，同时分析学习流程各阶段对系统性问题解决的影响。

Result: 模型展现强空间迁移能力但在长度扩展上持续失败（源于递归不稳定性）；数据覆盖设定能力极限；强化学习改善训练稳定性但不扩展极限；推理时优化提升性能但无法解决长度扩展失败。

Conclusion: 系统性泛化的失败可以归因于特定因素，不同学习阶段对问题解决能力有差异化影响，模型能力存在根本性限制，某些类型的泛化失败无法通过现有方法（如推理时优化）来弥补。

Abstract: Whether language models can systematically generalize remains actively debated. Yet empirical performance is jointly shaped by multiple factors such as training data, training paradigms, and inference-time strategies, making failures difficult to interpret. We introduce a controlled synthetic environment based on shortest-path planning, a canonical composable sequential optimization problem. The setup enables clean separation of these factors and supports two orthogonal axes of generalization: spatial transfer to unseen maps and length scaling to longer-horizon problems. We find that models exhibit strong spatial transfer but consistently fail under length scaling due to recursive instability. We further analyze how distinct stages of the learning pipeline influence systematic problem-solving: for example, data coverage sets capability limits; reinforcement learning improves training stability but does not expand those limits; and inference-time scaling enhances performance but cannot rescue length-scaling failures.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [Switching Efficiency: A Novel Framework for Dissecting AI Data Center Network Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14690)
*Niangen Ye,Jiawen Zhu,Baojun Chen,Dong Wang,Jiang Sun,Weiqiang Sun,Weisheng Hu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了交换效率框架（Switching Efficiency Framework），通过核心指标η及其三个分解因子（数据、路由效率、端口利用率）来量化LLM训练中AI数据中心网络的通信效率，并分析了不同网络架构和设计选择对通信效率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网络指标无法将网络活动直接关联到计算进度，且缺乏足够的粒度来诊断不同网络设计模式的影响，因此无法有效指导资本密集型AI数据中心集群的设计。

Method: 提出交换效率框架，定义核心指标交换效率（η）来量化单位交换容量下的计算有效数据吞吐量，并将η分解为三个因子：数据、路由效率和端口利用率，以便分析不同的通信瓶颈。

Result: 分析了3D-Torus的对称分布式交换和Rail-Optimized架构的集中式分层交换如何适应稀疏或不平衡的LLM训练流量；发现Mixture-of-Experts模型的All-to-All流量严重降低了端口利用率和路由效率；验证了调整交换资源分配、扩展服务器规模、采用网络内计算和多平面设计等关键设计选择对通信效率的积极影响。

Conclusion: 交换效率框架为分析效率瓶颈提供了有效的分析工具，能够指导未来一代AI数据中心网络的设计决策。

Abstract: Communication is pivotal in LLM training, and a thorough analysis of the communication efficiency of AI data center (AIDC) network is essential for guiding the design of these capital-intensive clusters. However, conventional metrics are inadequate for such analysis, as they do not directly link network activity to computational progress and lack granularity to diagnose the impact of different network design patterns. To address this, we introduce a metric framework, the Switching Efficiency Framework, whose core metric - Switching Efficiency ($η$) - quantifies computationally effective data throughput per unit switching capacity. We further decompose $η$ into three factors - Data, Routing Efficiency, and Port Utilization to facilitate analysis of distinct communication bottlenecks.
  Using this metric framework, we demonstrate how the symmetric, distributed switching of 3D-Torus and the centralized, hierarchical switching of Rail-Optimized architecture align with sparse or imbalanced LLM training traffic, and show that All-to-All traffic from Mixture-of-Experts models severely degrades their port utilization and routing efficiency. Our analysis also demonstrates how key design choices - such as adjusting switching resource allocation, expanding server size, adopting in-network computing, and multi-plane design - positively influence distinct facets of communication efficiency. Ultimately, the Switching Efficiency Framework provides an analytical tool for analyzing efficiency bottlenecks, thereby informing the design of future-generation AIDC networks.

</details>


### [188] [MLDAS: Machine Learning Dynamic Algorithm Selection for Software-Defined Networking Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14957)
*Pablo Benlloch,Oscar Romero,Antonio Leon,Jaime Lloret*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种将机器学习算法与SDN控制器动态集成的网络安全框架，通过自适应模型选择机制，根据实时网络流量特征自动选择最合适的ML算法，以提升SDN环境下入侵检测系统的鲁棒性和检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全是当今数字环境的关键问题，用户对安全浏览体验和个人数据保护的需求日益增长。现有的SDN攻击检测机制存在局限性，需要一种能够适应动态网络条件、保持可靠入侵检测能力的增强型解决方案。

Method: 提出了一个自适应模型选择框架，将机器学习算法与SDN控制器动态集成。该机制能够根据观测到的网络流量特征，实时动态选择最适合的ML算法。研究分析了基于流量类型的指标来定义有效的分类规则，并通过超参数调优来解决过拟合和欠拟合问题，优化模型准确性和泛化能力。

Result: 开发了一种自动化机制，能够根据实时网络条件自适应选择最合适的ML算法。该方法在变化的网络条件下能够维持可靠的入侵检测性能，优先保证检测的鲁棒性和SDN环境下的操作可行性。

Conclusion: 动态自适应ML算法选择机制能够有效增强SDN环境下的网络安全，通过根据网络流量特征实时调整检测模型，解决了传统静态方法的局限性，为构建更安全、更具适应性的网络通信系统提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Network security is a critical concern in the digital landscape of today, with users demanding secure browsing experiences and protection of their personal data. This study explores the dynamic integration of Machine Learning (ML) algorithms with Software-Defined Networking (SDN) controllers to enhance network security through adaptive decision mechanisms. The proposed approach enables the system to dynamically choose the most suitable ML algorithm based on the characteristics of the observed network traffic. This work examines the role of Intrusion Detection Systems (IDS) as a fundamental component of secure communication networks and discusses the limitations of SDN-based attack detection mechanisms. The proposed framework uses adaptive model selection to maintain reliable intrusion detection under varying network conditions. The study highlights the importance of analyzing traffic-type-based metrics to define effective classification rules and enhance the performance of ML models. Additionally, it addresses the risks of overfitting and underfitting, underscoring the critical role of hyperparameter tuning in optimizing model accuracy and generalization. The central contribution of this work is an automated mechanism that adaptively selects the most suitable ML algorithm according to real-time network conditions, prioritizing detection robustness and operational feasibility within SDN environments.

</details>


### [189] [Expanding into Reality: Random Graphs for Datacenter Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.15261)
*Giacomo Bernardi,Ratul Mahajan,C. Seshadhri,Enrico Carlesso,Chinchu Merine Joseph,Saurabh Kumar,Pavan Manikonda,Luiza Popa,Randy Ram,Steven Robinson,Elizabeth Tennent*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Amazon设计并部署的首个基于随机图的生产级数据中心网络架构RNG，通过新型分布式路由协议和无源光缆混洗设备，在成本降低45%的同时，性能达到或超越传统胖树架构，已成为Amazon多数工作负载的默认方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管随机图拓扑在成本和容错性方面具有长期已知的优势，但缺乏可扩展的路由和布线方案阻碍了其实际应用。为解决这一关键障碍，研究团队致力于实现随机图拓扑在大型生产数据中心中的实际部署。

Method: 提出RNG架构，包含两个核心创新：1）设计新型分布式路由协议，利用随机图属性在端点对之间发现大量边不相交路径；2）开发新型无源光器件，通过内部混洗电缆端点，将布线复杂度降至与胖树架构相当的水平。

Result: RNG架构实现成本比胖树架构降低高达45%，并在多种流量模式下性能匹配或超越胖树。该架构已成功部署于Amazon生产环境，成为多数工作负载的默认数据中心网络架构。

Conclusion: 通过创新的分布式路由协议和布线技术，成功克服了随机图拓扑实际部署的关键障碍，证明了随机图架构在大规模生产数据中心中的可行性和优越性，为数据中心网络设计提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We design and deploy at Amazon the first production datacenter fabrics based on random graphs. While the cost and fault-tolerance benefits of such topologies have been long known, their practical realization has been hampered by a lack of scalable routing and cabling approaches. Our design, called RNG, has a new distributed routing protocol that exploits the properties of random graphs to find a large number of edge disjoint paths between endpoint pairs. A novel passive optical device that internally shuffles cable endpoints makes Amazon's cabling complexity similar to that of fat trees. We show that RNG fabrics match or exceed the performance of fat trees for a range of traffic patterns, despite being up to 45% cheaper. At Amazon, we made RNG the default datacenter fabric for most workloads.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [FedGUI: Benchmarking Federated GUI Agents across Heterogeneous Platforms, Devices, and Operating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14956)
*Wenhao Wang,Haoting Shi,Mengying Yuan,Yiquan Lin,Panrong Tong,Hanzhang Zhou,Guangyi Liu,Pengxiang Zhao,Yue Wang,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出FedGUI，首个用于联邦学习GUI智能体训练的跨平台基准测试，涵盖移动端、网页端和桌面端，通过六大数据集系统研究了跨平台、跨设备、跨操作系统和跨数据源四种关键异质性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统集中式方法训练GUI智能体面临高昂成本和可扩展性挑战，而联邦学习虽能解决这些问题，却缺乏能够捕捉真实世界跨平台异质性的基准测试。

Method: 构建FedGUI基准测试框架，提供六个精选数据集，系统性研究四种异质性维度：跨平台、跨设备、跨操作系统和跨数据源，并在多个平台上进行广泛实验。

Result: 实验表明：（1）跨平台协作能提升性能，验证了联邦学习可从单一移动平台扩展到多样化GUI环境；（2）识别出平台和操作系统是最具影响力的异质性因素。

Conclusion: FedGUI为社区提供了重要基础，有助于构建更具可扩展性和隐私保护性的GUI智能体，推动真实世界部署应用。代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Training GUI agents with traditional centralized methods faces significant cost and scalability challenges. Federated learning (FL) offers a promising solution, yet its potential is hindered by the lack of benchmarks that capture real-world, cross-platform heterogeneity. To bridge this gap, we introduce FedGUI, the first comprehensive benchmark for developing and evaluating federated GUI agents across mobile, web, and desktop platforms. FedGUI provides a suite of six curated datasets to systematically study four crucial types of heterogeneity: cross-platform, cross-device, cross-OS, and cross-source. Extensive experiments reveal several key insights: First, we show that cross-platform collaboration improves performance, extending prior mobile-only federated learning to diverse GUI environments; Second, we demonstrate the presence of distinct heterogeneity dimensions and identify platform and OS as the most influential factors. FedGUI provides a vital foundation for the community to build more scalable and privacy-preserving GUI agents for real-world deployment. Our code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/wwh0411/FedGUI..
